Tooltip delay as the layers panel's.

### 2026-11-24 Canvas view: the task bar can be moved (#381, epic #231)

- Control: `canvasTaskGrip` (a `MoveGrip`, in `canvasTaskGripBox`, 4u by 7u, first in the bar's top row, glyph `⋮⋮`, tooltip "Drag to move"); in `canvasTaskMoreMenu` after a hairline: **Pin position** (checked while pinned; enabled once the bar has been moved) and **Reset position** (enabled once it has been moved). State: `App.canvasActions.barPlace` (`src/canvas/task_bar_place.h`), kept in `canvas-task-bar.json` of the state folder.
- Old: the bar always floated under the selection (or across the foot), with no way to put it elsewhere.
- New: Photoshop's Contextual Task Bar (References: its grip moves the bar, its "..." menu has Pin and Reset, from Adobe's public documentation; no asset or text copied). A drag moves the bar anywhere over the picture, 4u clear of the edges, pulled lightly (3u) to the edges and the middle, and it stays where it was dropped, across restarts. Its place is kept as fractions of the room it has (`engine/float_place.h`, as the run toolbar's), so after a resize it keeps its relative place and can never lie outside the area (a bar wider than the area is centred). **Reset position** forgets the place: the bar floats under the selection again. **Pin position** locks it: the grip is dimmed and its tooltip says how to unpin. In a narrow area (the bar docked at the foot) the kept place is ignored, not lost. Focus order and shortcuts are unchanged: the grip takes no Tab stop.
- Why: the owner's request (2026-11-24): the bar covers the part of the picture being edited. Fitts: the grip is the bar's first and nearest edge, 4u by 7u (28px high). Learned place: Photoshop's. The model holds the clamping, so it is tested without a window.
- Deviations, each to confirm: Photoshop's "Hide bar" is not built (the bar already hides without a selection, and View > Bars and panels is a later slice); a drag remembers the place at once (Photoshop's unpinned bar returns to the selection); here Pin only protects the place from an accidental drag, since the place is kept anyway; the place is one for the whole program, not per window; no keyboard move (the grip has no Tab stop, to keep the focus order).

### 2026-11-22 Canvas view: the task bar (#231 slice 1, epics #206 and #216)

- Control: `src/canvas/CanvasTaskBar.qml` in the Canvas view (both hosts), over the picture: `canvasTaskBox` (the bar: `popup` fill, `hairline` edge, `popupCorner`, level 2 shadow, 2u inside, at most 150u wide) holding `canvasTaskAction` (the action menu `canvasTaskActionMenu`: Auto, then the actions; a broken one disabled with its reason as the row's note), `canvasTaskPrompt` (a 7u field on `well`, its hint the field's placeholder (2026-11-27; it was a `canvasTaskPromptHint` text of its own), the clock `canvasTaskHistory` inside it at the end, the list `canvasTaskHistoryMenu`), `canvasTaskGenerate` or `canvasTaskCancel`, `canvasTaskMore` (`canvasTaskMoreMenu`: Clear prompt history…, then the question `canvasTaskClearAsk` with `canvasTaskClearKeep` and `canvasTaskClearYes`); under them `canvasTaskStatusRow` (`canvasTaskStatus`, `canvasTaskDownload`, `canvasTaskBackToLatest`), only when there is something to say; `canvasTaskShimmer` on the selection while a run goes. Glyphs `sparkles`, `wand-sparkles`, `history`, `download` added to the subset.
- Old: none (a canvas edit needed a workflow in the Graph and Run).
- New: Photoshop's Contextual Task Bar (References): the bar under the selection, 2u clear, above it when there is no room, 4u inside the picture, following a pan or zoom; across the foot of the picture when the picture is narrower than 72u (a small area: a squeezed bar would hide its own controls). It shows only with a selection (the rectangle, or a Quick Mask selection while Quick Mask is on) or while its runs or its last word last: no technical text over the work otherwise (Cohesion checklist). Order from the start: the action menu (its glyph alone below 90u of bar), the prompt (fills the row), Generate (the primary, last of the controls), `⋯` (flat, at the very end, as a bar's overflow). One status line, in `label` (`error` for an error), with at most one action button after it (compact).
- Why: the owner's product principle (a hobbyist edits the canvas without seeing a node; a film team keeps the workflow) and the design (docs/canvas-actions-design.md 4). Anchored to the selection (Fitts: the prompt is where the eye and the pointer already are, and the result appears just above it); one row and one line keep the picture visible.
- Rules met: Floor (28px buttons and field; the clock 24px inside the field, its 24px circle meeting no other target), Primary (Generate the one accent fill; Cancel is plain), Destructive (Clear prompt history… asks first, Keep highlighted and focused, Clear in the danger tint after the 4u gap, D8; nothing in the bar deletes), Gaps (1u inside the row, the status line 1u under it, 2u between its text and its button), Order (Cancel takes Generate's place, last in its chunk), Words (Generate, Cancel, Download, Back to latest as History's, Clear prompt history… with an ellipsis because a question follows; "the latest version", never Head or a hash), Keys (Enter in the prompt generates; Ctrl+Enter stays `run.start`, owner decision 7; Escape in the field or the view cancels the bar's runs while they go, else it is the window's or the dialog's; Up and Down step through the history; the bare letters are the field's while it has the focus, `belongsToTextField`), Keyboard (Tab reaches the menu, the field, the clock, Generate and `⋯` in reading order), Right-to-left (the bar mirrors, the picture and the shimmer do not), Posture (part of a sovereign view; the menus and the question are transient).
- Deviations, each to confirm: the bar is not shown without a selection (the design centres it at the foot; the task brief and the cohesion rule say no text over the work unless needed to act); no hide yet (Photoshop has it; a later slice with View > Bars and panels); grip, pin and reset: 2026-11-24, above; the shimmer is marching dashes on the frame, not a gradient, and does not yet stay still under a reduced-motion setting (Fugue has none yet). Pictures: `tests/canvas/test_canvas_actions_window.cpp` with `FUGUE_SHOT_DIR` (missing models, ready, generating, error, a narrow area; dark and light).

### 2026-11-23 Canvas view: Live in the task bar (#315)

- Control: `canvasTaskLive`, a flat toggle button "Live" after Advanced in the task bar; "Another preview" in the More menu.
- Old: the picture was redrawn only when Generate was pressed.
- New: with Live on, a change of the canvas, the selection or the prompt, once still (600 ms, longer after a slow run), re-runs the action as a preview inside the selection (the live frame); the stale run is cancelled at once. It writes no layer; Generate (Enter) keeps the preview as a layer through the same seed. A failed preview switches Live off with the one-line error. Live is off in every new session and goes off when the canvas changes; it is disabled, with a tip, while another canvas is live.
- Why: preview before commit costs the GPU, so it is a deliberate toggle (error prevention); the preview is where the eye is, and keeping it is the primary button the person already knows (one Generate, one layer, one undo step). Text on the button, not a glyph: no glyph reads as "live" without a label (recognition).
- Deviations, each to confirm: no key shortcut yet; no fast preview setting per action (design 16.5).

### 2026-11-23 Canvas view: the sampler's live frame inside the selection (live preview item 2)

- Control: `CanvasLiveFill.qml` (`canvasLiveFill`) in the Canvas view, between the picture and the region frame; `canvasTaskShimmer` drops to half opacity while a frame shows. No new setting: it follows the Sampler preview setting.
- Old: a canvas action showed "Generating… 45%" and the marching outline, no picture until the layer landed.
- New: Photoshop's generative-fill feel: the newest step frame is drawn on the padded context box and clipped to the selection, above the layers, under the selection's frame; the first frame replaces the quiet picture; a good end fades it over 200 ms into the layer (no flash), a cancel or an error removes it at once; other runs' previews are ignored; `step_preview` Off leaves the shimmer alone.
- Why: the owner's live-preview decision (Opus design review): feedback where the eye already is (Fitts), and the frame is clipped to the selection so the unchanged padding never appears to change.
- Deviations, each to confirm: the outline is dimmed, not removed, while a frame shows; no reduced-motion variant yet (Fugue has no such setting).

### 2026-11-23 Canvas view: Upscale in the task bar (#231 slice 8)

- Control: the action menu lists Upscale and Upscale (no model). No new control: the bar shows with nothing selected while one of them is chosen (like Remove background), at the foot of the picture, and Scale (2x or 4x) is a row of word buttons in the Advanced popover, with the model picker for Upscale. The prompt hint reads "No prompt needed: press Generate". When the run ends the view that showed the source switches to the new canvas and the notice says "Made a new canvas: the original is as it was (File > Open canvas...)."
- Old: no upscale in the bar.
- New: one click makes a bigger copy of the whole picture as a canvas of its own; a selection is not read.
- Why: a canvas keeps its size and its revisions are immutable, so a bigger picture is a new canvas (design 14, open question 4); Scale lives in Advanced as Refine's Strength does, so the simple state stays Action, Prompt, Generate.
- Deviations, each to confirm: the button still says Generate; Scale is not on the bar itself; the view switches to the new canvas at once (the source stays one File > Open canvas away).

### 2026-11-23 Canvas view: Remove background in the task bar (#231 slice 6)

- Control: the action menu lists Remove background. A missing pack shows in the status row the line "Needs the Remove Background pack" and a compact `ViewButton` `canvasTaskInstallPack` ("Install…", download glyph) before the Download button; Generate is disabled with the tip "Install the pack first". For an action whose selection is optional the bar also shows with nothing selected while that action is chosen (at the foot of the picture); with no single current layer the tip reads "Select one layer first".
- Old: the bar showed only with a selection (or while Expand was chosen); a missing node pack made an action broken with a node-type reason.
- New: an optional-selection action needs no selection, so the bar stays up like Expand's; a missing pack is an offer, not a fault, in the same status row and place as the missing-models line (Install… takes the Download button's place: models wait for the pack).
- Why: one click on the current layer (Photoshop's Remove Background); consent stays with the window (Install… opens Settings > Packs, which asks).
- Rules met: Floor (ViewButton), Words ("Install…" ends in an ellipsis because a dialog follows), Primary (Generate stays the one accent), Keyboard (Tab and Enter).
- Deviations, each to confirm: reaching the action with nothing selected takes one selection first (Auto never picks it); the button still says Generate; no entry in the Layers panel menu yet.

### 2026-11-23 Canvas view: Refine, Remove and Expand in the task bar (#231 slices 5 and 7)

- Control: the action menu lists three more actions (no new control for Refine and Remove). For an action whose selection is `bounds` (Expand) the bar gets one more row, `canvasTaskExpandRow`: the label "Grow the canvas" and a `ViewButton` (`canvasTaskExpand`, "Right, 25%") that opens `canvasTaskExpandMenu` (Left, Right, Top, Bottom, each at 25% and 50%). While Expand is the chosen action the bar shows with nothing selected (at the foot of the picture), because Expand has no selection of its own; the prompt hint reads "Describe the scene, or leave empty". For an action with no prompt role (Remove) the hint reads "No prompt needed: press Generate".
- Old: only Generative fill; the bar shown with a selection only.
- New: the row is a second line of the bar, shown only for Expand (Hick: the simple state of the other actions stays Action, Prompt, Generate, `⋯`); the side and amount are one menu of eight rows rather than two controls (two choices, eight rows, a short list: no search); the amount is a share of the picture's own side so it means the same on any size.
- Why: Expand has to say how far; the frame cannot yet leave the picture, so the bar says it.
- Rules met: Floor (28px menu rows, ViewButton), Words (Left, Right, Top, Bottom, "Grow the canvas": the same sides as Crop), Primary (Generate stays the one accent), Keyboard (the button and its menu are reached with Tab and Enter).
- Deviations, each to confirm: the row stays up with nothing selected only while Expand is chosen; one side per run; no preview of the new area on the picture before Generate (a dashed outline of the grown canvas is the follow-up when the frame can leave the picture).

### 2026-11-23 Canvas view: the task bar's Advanced popover, Count and Refine's presets (#231 slice 4)

- Control: `src/canvas/CanvasTaskAdvanced.qml` (`canvasTaskAdvanced`, a `Popup` on the overlay under the bar's new **Advanced** button `canvasTaskAdvancedButton`, flat, the glyph `settings` alone below 90u; rows `canvasTaskAdvancedRow-<key>` with the Graph's `InputWidget` `canvasTaskAdvancedWidget-<key>` or, for an input with `choice_values`, one `ViewButton` per word `canvasTaskAdvancedChoice-<key>-<word>`; `canvasTaskCount-1` to `canvasTaskCount-4` in `canvasTaskCountRow`; `canvasTaskAdvancedReset`; `canvasTaskEdit`), and the bar's status line (`canvasTaskStatus`: "Generating 2 of 3… 45%"). `canvasTaskMoreMenu` keeps only Clear prompt history….
- Old: the bar's only way to a value other than the prompt was Edit workflow and the Graph; Edit workflow sat in `⋯`; Refine's strength was a slider in the workflow only; one Generate made one layer.
- New: **Advanced** opens a transient popover hung from the button (right edge on the button's, under it, above it when there is no room). It lists what the action binds that the bar has no field for (strength, model, steps, plain inputs), each with the widget a node face uses: the drag number, the model picker with its Download rows, the choice. A word input (Refine: Light, Default, Restyle) is a row of buttons, the one chosen in the quiet marking. Under them **Count** 1 to 4 (only for an action with a seed) and **Edit workflow** (moved here from `⋯`). **Reset** appears once something differs from the action's own values. Values stay per action and per project. With Count above 1, one Generate makes that many layers, "<name> (1 of 3)" and so on, the first shown and the rest hidden; the bar says which one is going; Cancel (or Escape) ends them all.
- Why: design 4 (the simple state stays Action, Prompt, Generate; the rest is one press away), Hick (three words are a smaller choice than a 0.3 to 1 slider and say what they do), and the film team's rule that the Graph's widgets mean the same everywhere (a model picked here is the picker of the Graph).
- Rules met: Floor (the buttons are 28px `ViewButton`s, the widget rows 28px), Words ("Advanced", "Count", "Reset", "Edit workflow"), Primary (Generate stays the one accent; the chosen word and count are the quiet marking), Keyboard (Escape closes the popover; the buttons take Tab), Right-to-left (the popover mirrors with the bar's layout), Learned place (the model picker, drag number and its Download are the Graph's).
- Deviations, each to confirm: the button is a text button on the bar's row (the design said a popover from `⋯` or a chevron); Count goes to 4, not 8 (the instruction of this slice; one constant); no `‹ 2 of 3 ›` strip yet, the other variations are shown by their eyes in the Layers panel; the licence line of a non-commercial model is not in the popover (the model picker's Download question shows it).

### 2026-11-23 Canvas view: actions from the library and Edit workflow in the task bar (#231 slice 2)

- Control: `canvasTaskMoreMenu` gains **Edit workflow** (first row, value `edit`; its note names the action: "Open Generative fill as a workflow in the Graph"), above Clear prompt history…. The action menu `canvasTaskActionMenu` now lists every template of the library that carries an `action` block, with the bundled and own ones in one list; it is read again each time it opens. No new control is added to the bar's row.
- Old: the menu held only the actions compiled into Fugue; the only way to the action's graph was to find the template in Browse templates.
- New: Edit workflow opens the chosen action (Auto: Generative fill) as an unsaved tab of the Graph (a copy: Save asks where, a bundled file is never written) and makes the Graph's area the active one; in the dialog host, which hides the Graph, the dialog closes first. In the overflow menu, not on the bar, because it is what the one who builds reaches for, not the one who types a prompt (Hick: the simple state stays Action, Prompt, Generate, `⋯`); the design puts it in the Advanced popover, which is a later slice, and this row moved there with slice 4 (the Advanced popover).
- Why: design 7 (the pro loop: Graph for what happens, Canvas for where). A hobbyist never sees it; a film team has it one click from the bar.
- Rules met: Floor (the menu row is 28px as every WidgetMenu row), Words ("Edit workflow", no ellipsis: no dialog follows, the tab is the result), Order (before the destructive row, which stays last), Primary (no new accent control), Keyboard (a menu entry, reached with the `⋯` button).
- Deviations, each to confirm: Edit workflow sits in `⋯` until the Advanced popover exists; the bar runs the saved action, not the tab's unsaved edits (decision 6 follows with the tab-driven bar); a saved copy does not yet replace the bundled one in the menu (saveOwn, next slice).

### 2026-11-22 Canvas view: one status line, the rest in tooltips and Advanced details (#234)

- Control: `src/canvas/CanvasView.qml`: the status line `canvasViewStatusLine` (`canvasViewStatusZoom`, `canvasViewStatusSelection`, `canvasViewAdvancedDetails`, `canvasViewStatusDetails`), the context box's label `canvasViewContextLabel` and the frame's tooltip (`canvasViewFrameHover`); removed: `canvasViewSummary`, `canvasViewRegionInfo`, the how-to text of `canvasViewRegionHint`, and `canvasViewRegionNote` with `canvasViewUseRegion` except for a pinned node. `CompareView.qml` (`showSize`).
- Old: four lines of internal text under the picture (the store diff of the shown step, what the node reads with Use for region, the context paragraph, the how-to), and a size and zoom chip over the picture.
- New: Photoshop's one status line at the foot: "32% · 2000 × 2000", then the dragged or set rectangle's "456 × 777 at 0, 253" (live), and **Advanced details** (flat toggle, at the end) for the context and model size, what the node reads, the revision and the diff. "Context 456 × 777" is a small label at the dashed box's corner; the model's size is in the frame's tooltip. "Canvas Region reads …" and **Use for region** appear only when the node is pinned to a step other than the latest version. The how-to is gone: the tool strip's tooltips teach the drag (no first-use hint kept). The "Layer <name> added" notice with Undo stays.
- Why: the owner ("Not sure this is needed either"). Hobbyists see the picture and one quiet line; film teams open Advanced details. Only what a person must act on (linked inputs, Compare on, an unsaved rectangle, errors) stays as a line.
- Rules met: Floor (the toggle is a 28px-high compact `ViewButton`; the line is a readout, not a target), Primary (none), Destructive (none), Gaps (3u between readouts), Learned place (status at the foot, as Photoshop), Right-to-left (the line mirrors, the picture does not), No technical text around the work (the new checklist line).
- Deviations: the line is a readout (caption size), so it is not a 28px row; the toggle is the one control in it.

### 2026-11-22 Question dialogs: one button, one answer row (#235)

- Control: every Question's answers (`ThemedButton`), the quit question (`quitAsk`, now a `Question`), `QuestionAnswers.qml`, the close-tab, trust, recover, group, delete-group and model-download questions, the Templates dialog's Download and Cancel.
- Old: `ThemedButton` was a Material `Button` (48px, no fill or edge on the offscreen and themed look, the label 14px, light-on-light for a highlighted answer in a light theme); the quit question was a bare `Popup` with its own padding and no focus rule; `Discard` was untinted; rows used literal 12 and 16px gaps.
- New: `ThemedButton` is a thin alias of `ViewButton` (D4; `highlighted` is `primary`, `danger`, `flat`, `bordered` unless flat), as `SmallButton` is, so a question's answers are 28px, the hairline edge, `smallCorner`, `labelSize`, the 2px accent focus ring and the same hover and pressed fills as Settings and Projects. `Question` sets the window's `textSize` for its text; `QuestionAnswers` is the one row (Cancel or the safe answer first, the primary last at the right, `2u` gap, `4u` before a destructive answer, or the destructive answer alone at the far side with `leadingSpacer: false`). Quit: Keep working (the safe answer, the primary, focused: Enter and Escape keep working) then Quit in the danger tint. Close tab: Discard (danger, far side), Cancel, Save (primary, focused: Enter saves). Recover: Discard (danger), Recover. Delete group: Cancel (primary, focused), Delete (danger).
- Rules met: Floor (28px), Primary (one per question), Destructive and D8 (danger tint, its own chunk, the safe answer highlighted and focused), D9 (primary last at the right), D4, Right-to-left (the row mirrors with the content).
- Pictures: `tests/canvas/test_question_dialogs.cpp` with `FUGUE_SHOT_DIR`, dark and light, beside Settings.
- 2026-11-27: `QuestionAnswers` is replaced by `KitDialogButtons` (the row above); the close-tab question now reads Cancel, Discard, Save.

### 2026-11-22 Canvas view: the tool strip, the mask brush and Quick Mask (#230, epics #206 and #216)

- Control: `src/canvas/CanvasView.qml`: the strip `canvasViewToolStrip` (a 7u column at the start of the picture, 2u from it: `canvasViewTool-hand`, `canvasViewTool-zoom` · `canvasViewTool-region` · `canvasViewTool-brush`, `canvasViewTool-eraser` · `canvasViewQuickMask`), the options bar `canvasViewOptions` between the bar and the picture (`canvasViewToolName`; `canvasViewSnap`, moved here from the bar; `canvasViewZoomIn`, `canvasViewZoomOut`; `canvasViewBrushTarget` with `canvasViewBrushTargetText`, or `canvasViewBrushAddMask` and `canvasViewBrushQuickMask`; `canvasViewBrushSize`, `canvasViewBrushHardness`, `canvasViewBrushOpacity`; `canvasViewMaskOverlay`, `canvasViewMaskMenu`; `canvasViewUseSelection`; `canvasViewClearSelection`; `canvasViewSelectionProblem`), the masks over the picture `canvasViewLayerMask` and `canvasViewSelection` (MaskLayer), the pointer `canvasViewBrushPointer` with `canvasViewBrushBrushRing` (`src/canvas/BrushPen.qml`, now also the Mask editor's and the paint window's pointer), `canvasViewZoomTool`. `src/canvas/CanvasLayersPanel.qml`: `canvasLayerAddMask`, the row menu's Add mask and Paint the mask, a click on `canvasLayerMask-<n>` (an accent frame while the brush paints it). Commands `canvas-view.*` in the new `canvas-view` context (spec 12.4). Glyphs `hand`, `zoom-in`, `circle-dashed` added to the subset.
- Old: one tool, the region frame; a layer mask could be turned off or deleted but not painted; no selection other than the rectangle.
- New: Photoshop's toolbar order and keys, Blender's narrow toolbar: Hand (H; Space held for a moment), Zoom (Z; click in, Alt+click out), then Rectangle region (M or R, the default: today's frame), then Mask brush (B) and eraser (E; X swaps), then Quick Mask (Q) at the foot, as Photoshop's. 28px glyph buttons 1u apart, chunks 4u apart; the current tool and Quick Mask in the quiet marking. The options bar is one 7u row (it wraps in a narrow area): the tool's name in `label`, then its settings; for the brush what it paints (a chip "Mask of <layer>" or "Selection", or Add mask and Quick Mask when there is nothing to paint on), Size, Hardness and Opacity as drag-number fields (30u), the layer mask's Overlay toggle (\) and its menu (Invert, Ctrl+I; Reveal all; Hide all), Quick Mask's Use selection and, apart and last, Clear selection (danger). Add mask (layers panel button, row menu, Ctrl+Shift+M) gives the selected layer a fully revealing mask as one revision and the brush takes it, as Photoshop does; a click on a mask's thumbnail does the same for an existing mask. The strokes rest 400 ms and are then one revision (`canvas-view:mask-paint`); Ctrl+Z over the view takes the canvas back one revision while a mask is painted, undoes a Quick Mask stroke in Quick Mask, and is the document's (the region's) otherwise.
- Why: the owner's request (select a layer, add a mask, paint the area to inpaint; Photoshop's tool palette at the left). Photoshop's keys need no learning for its users; Blender's single narrow column keeps the picture large in a small area and a keyboard-first person never needs the strip. Tool settings sit in an options bar, not in a panel, so the Layers panel keeps its height and the settings are next to the picture they change (Fitts: a short way from the stroke to the size). The brush paints only something it can hold (a layer mask, the selection): this is not a paint program (#206's guard), so painting a layer's pixels is not offered.
- Rules met: Floor (every button 28px, the fields 28px), Primary (none in the strip or the options bar; Accept stays the view's one accent fill: Use selection is a plain button), Destructive (Clear selection in its own chunk, last, 4u from Use selection; Hide all is in a menu whose last row is not a removal; every mask edit is one revision Undo takes back, a Quick Mask stroke one step of its own undo), Gaps (1u inside, 4u between chunks in the strip and the bar), Learned place (the bar's order kept; Snap moved from the bar to the region's options, its own place now), Posture (the strip and the options are part of a sovereign view; the menu is transient). Keys: each checked against the registry: H (`view.focus-mode` in `canvas`, `canvas-layers.visible` over the panel), X, [ and ] (the panel's over the panel), M and \ (Compare Images' `compare` context, when a Compare node is selected in the Graph), Q (`app.quit`, which the view already kept from quitting, #228), Ctrl+Z and Ctrl+Shift+Z (`edit.undo`, `edit.redo`: forwarded when no mask is painted) are taken by the more specific `canvas-view` context while the pointer is over the view or it has the focus; over the layers panel the panel's keys still win. B, E, Z, R, Ctrl+I and Ctrl+Shift+M had no owner.
- Deviations from the references, and why (each to confirm): the brush **hides** on a layer mask (paints black) and the eraser shows, where Photoshop paints with the foreground color (white by default): on a fresh, fully revealing mask the first stroke must do something visible, and "red is what you painted" then holds for a layer mask and a Quick Mask alike. The overlay of a layer mask is **on** while the brush paints it (Photoshop's \ overlay is off by default): the view draws the composite per revision, not per dab, so the red is what shows a stroke live; \ turns it off to see the result, which follows each revision. Quick Mask's red marks the **selected** part (Photoshop's default marks the masked part; its option "Color indicates: Selected Areas" is this one): it is the part to change, as the Mask editor's paint is. The red is `look.error` at 50%, not the accent: Photoshop's rubylith, and the accent would read as a selection highlight. A selection is kept in the view while Quick Mask is off and drawn as marching ants (#313, 2026-11-23, below). The view's Ctrl+Z is routed by what is being done (above) rather than being one stack. Lasso, Move layer, Eyedropper, a layer mask as the selection (Ctrl+click its thumbnail) and painting a layer's own pixels are not built (the lasso and the layer mask as the selection: 2026-11-23, below).

### 2026-11-23 Canvas view: a selection limits the mask brush (#319)

- Control: no new control. `canvasViewLayerMask` (MaskLayer) takes `clipTo` (the Quick Mask / lasso selection `canvasViewSelection`) and `clipRect` (the region rectangle); the clip itself is `MaskPaint::setClip` (engine), applied in `MaskLayer::applyClip` before every brush, shape, invert, clear and fill.
- Old: the brush painted a layer mask everywhere, selection or not.
- New: Photoshop's rule: with a selection active, painting changes the layer mask only inside it, and a feathered edge weights the stroke by its value. A painted selection wins over the rectangle (as Modify selection does). Invert, Reveal all and Hide all follow the same clip. One revision per stroke group, as before.
- Why: Photoshop clips painting, fills and adjustments to the active selection; a selection that did not limit the brush would be a surprise to anyone who knows it. One clip in the engine, not in QML, so every paint path agrees.
- Rules met: no control added or moved, so Floor, Gaps and Posture are unchanged. Deviation to confirm: a selection is not shown differently while the brush is clipped (the ants already outline it).

### 2026-11-22 cohesion fixes, second batch: the Canvas view, its dialog, History, the layers panel (#228)

- Control: the audit's L1 to L18 and the decisions left for this batch (D3 in the view, D4, D5, D7 in the view, the Mask editor and the paint panel; D6 kept). `ViewButton.qml` (`bordered`, `flat`, `compact`, `trailingGlyph`, hover and pressed states), `SettingsParts.SmallButton` and `AreaHeader`'s `BarButton` (thin aliases), `CanvasView.qml` (`canvasViewCanvas`, `canvasViewPending`, chunks `canvasViewHistory` `canvasViewLayers` · `canvasViewOverlay` `canvasViewCompare` `canvasViewMode-<mode>` · `canvasViewSnap` · `canvasViewExport` · `canvasViewAccept` · `canvasViewDrop` · `canvasViewFit` `canvasViewActual` · the host's `trailing`; the banner `canvasViewNewResult` with `canvasViewNewBack`, `canvasViewNewAccept`, `canvasViewNewDrop`; `canvasViewUseRegion` "Use for region"; the empty state `canvasViewEmpty`, `canvasViewEmptyText`, `canvasViewEmptyNew`, `canvasViewEmptyImport`; `canvasViewContext`; the free selection's `canvasViewFreeRow`, `canvasViewAddRegion`, `canvasViewChooseRegion`, `canvasViewRegionChooser`; `canvasViewCanvasMismatch` with `canvasViewShowNodeCanvas` and `canvasViewUseCanvasForNode`; the notice `canvasViewHeadToast` with `canvasViewHeadToastUndo`; the History panel `canvasHistoryPanel`, `canvasHistoryShowAll`, `canvasHistoryList`, rows `canvasHistoryStep-<n>`, `canvasHistoryBackLatest`), `CanvasViewDialog.qml` (`canvasViewClose`), `LargeViewDialog.qml` (`showBar`, Home, right to left), `Question.qml` (right to left), `CanvasHistoryDialog.qml` (`canvasHistoryEmpty`, `canvasHistoryNew`, `canvasHistoryImport`, the shared modes, Enter), `CanvasLayersPanel.qml` (the eyes, `canvasLayersSolo` as a `Banner`, the Blend chevron), `CanvasArea.qml` (its ground and inset), `MaskEditor.qml` (`maskTool-<tool>`, `maskOnly`), `PaintLayersPanel.qml` (`paintLayerEye-<n>`), `CompareView.qml` (Home), the new components `CompareModeButtons.qml`, `Banner.qml`, `ContextBox.qml`, `NewCanvasQuestion.qml`, `canvasMenu.js`, `compareMemory.js`, and the commands `view.zoom-in` / `view.zoom-out` (`+`, `-`).
- Old: three rows of buttons in File > Canvas…; two accent Accepts and two Drops with the banner up; "Keep looking at head" beside Drop; "New result"; a node's id in the region line and the banner; text chevrons; four compare modes in the canvas surfaces against five in Compare Images; empty states naming `fugue canvas new`; the view's canvas menu without New or Import; an accent-tinted banner that pushed the picture down; toggles in the primary's accent; uneven chunk gaps; no mirroring in large views and questions; Home in a Canvas area fitting the Graph behind it; a drag on the picture doing nothing without a selected Canvas Region; "This region reads another canvas" in red for a node with no canvas yet.
- New: one bar (the canvas and the strip; then the tools in chunks 4u apart, ending in Fit, 100% and the dialog's close). The banner over the picture's foot, the notice look with a 3px accent bar (`Banner`): "New candidate <hash> from <the Canvas Write's title>", **Back to Head**, **Accept**, then **Drop** apart and last; the bar's Accept and Drop hide while it shows. Toggles, modes, the strip's shown chip, the Mask editor's current tool and Mask alone, the layers' eyes: the quiet marking. Compare Images' five modes everywhere, the last one remembered. The canvas menu is the picker's (name, size and time, the id; New canvas…, Import picture…), and an empty store offers the same two buttons ("No canvases yet") in the view and in History. "Use for region"; the region line names the node by its title ("Canvas Region reads Head"). Lucide chevrons (`trailingGlyph`). Keys: Home or 0 fits every picture and the Graph, `+` and `-` zoom both, Enter accepts a shown candidate; a focused Canvas view keeps the Graph's keys (and the bare Q) from the window. Right to left: the large views' bar and footer, the questions and the Canvas view mirror; pictures never. A Canvas view follows the selected Canvas Region, else the one last followed or chosen on this canvas, else the only one on this canvas; with none a drag still draws (view-only) and asks which node (several) or offers **Add a Canvas Region node** (none, one undo step); a node with no canvas takes the one shown at its first edit (one undo step with it); a node on another canvas says so in plain words with **Show <its canvas>** and **Use <this canvas> for this node**. A run's layer that lands on the Head (the normal flow, the owner's decision) is followed with a short notice "Layer <name> added" and **Undo**; the new-candidate banner is for real candidates only, without its hint line. The layers panel's read-only state says why by case and offers **Back to latest**, and for a candidate Accept (plain, not the accent) and Drop. **No strip, no hashes** (the owner: "all of this needs to disappear"): the strip of revision chips and Earlier are gone; the bar is the canvas, a `N pending` chip only while candidates wait, **History** (closed at first) and **Layers**, then the tools; the History panel lists the steps in words newest first (Photoshop's History panel is the reference: a step is a change named for a person, a click shows that state, Back to latest returns), with **All revisions** for the candidates; "the latest version" replaces Head in the view's text.
- Why: the audit's reference table (one job, one look); a selection is made by dragging on the picture, as in Photoshop, so the node is found for the person instead of the person hunting the node; Blender's rule that the keys belong to the focused editor; the owner's reports and decision (a run lands on the Head; deleting the layer or Undo is the review).
- Rules met: Floor (every button 28px, the area header's 24px), Primary (one accent fill on screen: the banner's Accept or the bar's, never both), Destructive (Drop last and in its own chunk, 4u from Accept against 1u inside; Add a Canvas Region node, Use … for this node and a drawn rectangle are one undo step each), Gaps (one chunk gap, 4u, in the tools; 1u inside), Learned place (no menu order changed; `+` and `-` were unbound; Home kept its Graph meaning and gained the pictures), Posture (the banner is inline over the picture, not a modal; the chooser is a transient menu).
- Decisions taken without the owner (each to confirm): History at the start side of the picture, Layers at the end (Photoshop stacks them in one column; two columns keep both lists tall in a wide view); a step is named from what changed in the layer list (a run's layer reads "Layer added: <its name>", which Canvas Write takes from the prompt), not from the producer, so a CLI edit and a view edit read alike; the History list shows the latest version's line only, the candidates under All revisions; the pending chip shows the newest candidate rather than opening a list (the History panel's All revisions lists them); the banner over the picture's foot, not its top (the top holds the label of what is shown); Back to Head first, then Accept, then Drop (Back first as Cancel is, Drop last as every destructive); a focused Canvas view keeps the bare Q from quitting (Q is the quick mask of the References; Ctrl+Q and the close still quit); Home no longer moves the compare divider; the follow target is kept while other nodes are selected (the frame is not lost when a prompt is edited); with no Region node a drawn rectangle is view-only until **Add a Canvas Region node** (honest: nothing keeps it otherwise); a new Region node is not given a canvas when it is created, only at its first edit in a view (no guess with no view open); the area draws the dialog's `popup` ground; the Head's notice goes by itself after 10 s and names the layers by comparing the layer names of the new Head with the old one (a renamed layer reads as added); the panel's Accept for a candidate is not the accent fill (the bar's is the primary).

### 2026-11-22 New canvas dialog (#232 slice A, epics #206, #216, #217)

- Control: File > New canvas… (`canvas.new`, Ctrl+Alt+N) and Paste as new canvas (`canvas.paste-new`, `src/menu/menus.cpp`, after Projects…, before Canvas…), and the dialog `src/canvas/NewCanvasDialog.qml` (`newCanvasDialog`; `newCanvasName`, `newCanvasSourceBlank`, `newCanvasSourceClipboard`, `newCanvasSourceFile`, `newCanvasPreset-<id>`, `newCanvasWidth`, `newCanvasHeight`, `newCanvasSourceLine`, `newCanvasBackground-<transparent|white|black|colour>`, `newCanvasColour`, `newCanvasColourPick`, `newCanvasPreview`, `newCanvasSummary`, `newCanvasProblem`, `newCanvasCancel`, `newCanvasOk`, `newCanvasFileDialog`). The canvas input's **New canvas…** (`WidgetPicker.qml`) opens it in place of the old question.
- Old: a 520px question with Name, a 1024 × 1024 size, a flat "From a picture…" button and Create; no presets, no background, no clipboard.
- New: Photoshop's New Document on a `Question` (560px max): Name; "Start from" Blank, Clipboard (on only while a picture is copied, with a tip "Copy a picture first" otherwise) and A file…; for a blank canvas the presets (Square, Landscape 4:3, Wide 16:9, Portrait 3:4, HD, 4K, Custom, 28px chips, the chosen one in the quiet marking), width × height (22u fields), and the background (Transparent, White, Black, Color with a hex field and the color picker); a 14u preview of the canvas's shape in its background beside one caption line with the size, megapixels and the cap; the reason in the error color; Cancel then **Create** (the one highlighted button) at the right, Enter in a field creates, Create waits while a reason is shown. A copied picture starts the dialog on Clipboard with its size in the (fixed) fields, as Photoshop's Clipboard preset does.
- Why: Photoshop's New Document is the reference (owner decision, 2026-11-22), so the order is the one people know: name, then what it starts from, then size and background, with the result summarized before the primary. Presets are chips, not a list: seven short words fit one or two rows and each is one click (Hick). The state of a choice is shown in the quiet marking so the accent stays Create's; the cap is said where the size is typed (visibility) and the error is live, so nothing is lost to a refusal on press. The last settings come back (recognition over recall), the name is never one the root has, and the source follows the clipboard so the common "I just copied a picture" is one click.
- Rules met: Floor (chips and buttons 28px, fields 28px), Primary (Create is the one highlighted button, last at the right, Cancel before it), Destructive (none: a canvas is never deleted), Gaps (3u between chunks, 2u inside the form, 1u between chips), Learned place (File menu order unchanged: the new rows sit after Projects…; Ctrl+Alt+N is free, Ctrl+N stays New workflow), Posture (a transient dialog).
- Unified: every New canvas… opens this dialog: File > New canvas…, a canvas input's menu, the Canvas view's canvas menu and empty state, the History dialog's empty state (`NewCanvasQuestion.qml` is retired). The Canvas view and History show the canvas made.

### 2026-11-23 Canvas view: the Layer menu and the Layer properties dialog (#252, epics #206 and #216)

- Control: the Canvas view's Layer menu rows `canvas-view.new-layer`, `.group`, `.ungroup`, `.merge-down`, `.flatten`, `.layer-properties` (were greyed placeholders); the layers panel's right-click menu (a "Combine" heading with New layer, Group layers, Ungroup layers, Merge down, Layer properties…); the dialog `canvasPropertiesAsk` in `src/canvas/CanvasFileDialogs.qml` with `canvasPropertiesName`, `canvasPropertiesOpacity` (`DragNumberField`), `canvasPropertiesBlend-<id>`, `canvasPropertiesMaskOn`, `canvasPropertiesMaskDelete`, `canvasPropertiesOk`.
- Old: the rows were disabled with "Not built yet (#252)"; a layer's name, opacity, blend and mask were separate controls of the panel, each its own revision.
- New: the rows are live and carry Photoshop's keys (Ctrl+Shift+N, Ctrl+E, Ctrl+G, Ctrl+Shift+G); a row is disabled (not hidden) when it does not apply: Merge down needs one selected layer with a shown paint layer or group under it, Ungroup needs groups only. The dialog is a `Question` (the window's one dialog form): a caption, Name (text field), Opacity (the panel's 28px drag-number field), Blend mode (a wrapping row of 28px buttons, the current one marked), the Layer mask row only for a masked layer (Mask on/off, and Delete mask in the danger tint: pressing it marks the mask for deletion and the button then reads Keep the mask, so nothing is lost before Apply), then Cancel and Apply (primary, last at the right). Enter in the name field applies. Everything the dialog changed is ONE revision, so one Undo takes it all back.
- Why: Photoshop's Layer Properties and Krita's layer properties dialog are the references (the owner's model: Photoshop's, Blender's interface); the panel keeps its quick controls for the common single change, the dialog is for several at once and for those who look for it in the menu (recognition over recall). Delete mask is deferred to Apply so the dialog stays a form that Cancel abandons whole.
- Rules met: Floor (every control 28px), Primary (Apply, one, last at the right; Cancel first), Destructive and D8 (Delete mask in the danger tint, apart from Mask on/off by the row's gap, and nothing is applied before Apply; every edit is one undo step), Gaps (1u inside a row, 3u between rows), Learned place (menu order as Photoshop's Layer menu: New, Group, Merge, Flatten, then properties), Right-to-left (the Question mirrors its content).

### 2026-11-23 Layers panel: lock, and Generate reads one layer (#226, epics #206 and #216)

- Control: in `src/canvas/CanvasLayersPanel.qml` a lock toggle in each row (`canvasLayerLock-<position>`) left of the eye, the row menu's Lock/Unlock and "Generate reads this layer only", and the notice `canvasLayersRegion` (with `canvasLayersRegionEnd`) under the list; the commands `canvas-layers.lock` (Ctrl+/) and `canvas-layers.use-in-region`; the task bar passes the layer to Generate.
- Old: no layer could be protected, so Delete, Merge down, Free transform and the mask brush took any layer; Generate always read the whole picture (the node's advanced Layer input was the only way to read one layer).
- New: the lock is a 28px toggle beside the eye, as Blender's outliner puts its toggles at the row's right; dim while open (0.4) and in the quiet marking while locked, never the accent fill. A locked layer cannot be deleted, merged (either side), flattened, moved or transformed, or have its mask painted, replaced or deleted: those controls are disabled in the panel and the menu (not hidden), and the store refuses the rest in plain words ("X is locked: it cannot be deleted. Unlock it first"). Showing, opacity, blend, a name, order and Duplicate stay open: they are not pixel edits. Lock is one revision (History says "Locked: X"), so Undo takes it back. "Generate reads this layer only" (a paint layer, one at a time) shows the same notice as Show alone ("Generate reads X only" and an x to go back), because it is the same kind of thing: a view state, not a revision. The task bar then sends that layer's name to Canvas Region's Layer input.
- Why: Photoshop's lock (Ctrl+/) and Blender's outliner toggles are the references (the owner's model). Lock sits at the row (Fitts: one click, next to the eye, no menu) and its effect is the same one rule everywhere, so a person never wonders which controls it covers. Reading one layer is the Photoshop habit of working from a layer and is quieter than a node input; the notice keeps it from being forgotten (visibility of state).
- Rules met: Floor (28px toggle; the notice's x is the compact flat button of the Show alone notice), Primary (none added), Destructive (the refusal is explicit and the unlock is one click; every change is a revision), Gaps (0.5u between the lock and the eye, as the eye's own margin), Learned place (the eye stays at the right edge; the name's width shrinks by one toggle), Posture (the panel stays dense; one extra notice row only while it applies). Keys: Ctrl+/ checked against the registry, free; `canvas-layers.use-in-region` has no key (a menu choice, like Show this layer alone).
- Padlock (#314): the row toggle shows Lucide's `lock` (U+E10F) while the layer, or its group, is locked and `lock-open` (U+E110) while it is open, in the same dim and lit states. Both were added to the bundled icon font with the subset command in `share/fonts/lucide-fugue.txt` (lucide-static 0.460.0, ISC, `share/fonts/LICENSE.lucide`).

### 2026-11-22 Canvas view: the layers panel (#226, epics #206 and #216)

- Control: `src/canvas/CanvasLayersPanel.qml` (`canvasLayersPanel`) at the right of the picture in `src/canvas/CanvasView.qml`, toggled by `canvasViewLayers` in the tools row (after 100%, before Show candidate). Header `canvasLayersUndo`, `canvasLayersRedo`; list `canvasLayerList`, rows `canvasLayer-<position>` with `canvasLayerThumb-<n>`, `canvasLayerMask-<n>`, `canvasLayerName-<n>` (`canvasLayerRename` while typed), `canvasLayerEye-<n>`, the drop line `canvasLayerDropLine`; under it `canvasLayersReadOnly`, `canvasLayersSolo` (`canvasLayersSoloEnd`), `canvasLayerOpacity` (`src/canvas/DragNumberField.qml`), `canvasLayerBlend`, `canvasLayerMaskToggle`, `canvasLayerMaskDelete`, `canvasLayerInfo`, `canvasLayerDuplicate`, `canvasLayerUp`, `canvasLayerDown`, `canvasLayerDelete`; the row menu (a `WidgetMenu`). `ViewButton` gains `altAction`/`altTapped` (an Alt+click of its own). New commands `canvas-layers.*` in the `canvas-layers` context (spec 12.4).
- Old: none; the Canvas view showed the flattened picture only, and a layer could be changed only in another program.
- New: a 56u (224px) well-colored panel, 2u from the picture, full height of it. A caption "Layers" with Undo and Redo (28px glyph buttons, flat) at its right. Rows 32px, 2px apart: 1u in, a 24px thumbnail on a checkerboard, 1u, a 24px mask thumbnail when the layer has a mask, the name (elided, 55% when hidden), the 28px eye at the right edge (0.5u in; lit while solo, flat and 55% when hidden). The selected rows in the selection color, a hovered row in the menu-row color, the focused list's current row with a 2px accent edge. A drag shows a 2px accent line in the gap. Under the list, 1u apart: the read-only note or the solo strip when they apply, then Opacity (a 28px drag-number field the panel's width: label left, value right, the accent fill for its share), Blend (a 28px flat button the panel's width, "Blend: Normal ▾"), for a masked layer Mask on/off and, 3u on, Delete mask (danger), the origin line (caption size), then Duplicate, up, down and, 3u on (more than twice the 1u inside), Delete (trash, danger).
- Why: the owner asked for Photoshop's model with Blender's interface. Simple first (Hick): eye, Opacity and Delete are visible without a menu; blend modes are one button, the mask's controls appear only on a masked layer, the rest is in the right-click menu and the keys, which the menu shows (recognition over recall). The eye sits at the right edge as in Blender's outliner, so the toggles line up in one column and a drag down that column (Fitts: a column of equal targets) reaches each; the thumbnails sit at the left as in Photoshop so a layer is found by its picture. The keys are the panel's only while the pointer is over it (Blender's hover rule), so H, X, Ctrl+D and Ctrl+Z over the panel act on layers and elsewhere keep their Graph meaning; the picture keeps the document's Ctrl+Z for the region (the region is a node input). A drag-number field gives the scrub, the typed value and the reset in one 28px target, without a slider's thin track. Solo is a view (no revision) because looking at one layer is not a change to the canvas.
- Rules met: Floor (every control 28px; rows 32px; thumbnails 24px are the row's press target, not their own except the mask's Shift+click, which is 24px), Primary (none: a properties panel decides nothing; the view's Accept stays the primary of its bar), Destructive (Delete is its own chunk, 3u from the arrange buttons against 1u inside; Delete mask 3u from Mask on/off; in the menu Delete is under its own heading and no delete is the last row; every edit is undone by the panel's Undo or Ctrl+Z), Gaps (1u inside a chunk, 3u before the destructive ones), Learned place (the paint window's layer list is unchanged; the outliner and Photoshop's panel are the references for the order: list, properties, arrange, delete; F2 renames as it does a node), Posture (a panel of a sovereign view: dense rows, no modal; the menu is transient). The hidden eye is the flat, dimmed button (the icon subset has no crossed eye), as in the paint window: a weaker cue, noted there too.

- Old: the findings N1 to N15 of the audit, and History comparing a candidate with its parent.
- New: every large view closes with the Lucide `x` ("Close (Esc)"), Fit and 100% name their keys; the notices' dismiss is the Lucide `x`; a `ViewButton` toggle says it is on to assistive tools. History: "Canvases" with a `chevron-left`, Compare and the modes in the quiet marking, rows hover in `menuRow`, the canvas list works by Up, Down and Enter, the tags read Head, Candidate, Dropped, and a candidate is compared with the head (the Canvas view's meaning). The region picker commits with OK; its Snap, its ratios and the Crop window's ratios are the quiet marking. The canvas menu's actions sit under a hairline (`WidgetMenu` separator, skipped while searching); the file dialog is "Import a picture as a canvas" (the menu's Import picture… and the CLI's `fugue canvas import`); the New canvas size fields are 22u. The area header's split buttons are the Lucide `columns-2` and `rows-2`, its close glyph turns red on focus too; the Graph's glyph is `waypoints` (the Workflows tab keeps `workflow`); the placeholder is `Text` at the label size. The menu bar's close has the 2px focus ring. Projects: Missing and Invalid capitalized as Current; Remove from list… in the danger tint; its question highlights Keep and puts Remove from list last, 3u apart; the forms put Back first and the primary last at the right. The Cache question highlights Cancel and puts Clear last in the danger tint. Settings say Graph navigation, "an empty part of the graph", Show new candidates.
- Why: the same job looks and acts the same in every surface (the audit's reference table); the decisions D1, D3, D7 to D11 of 2026-11-22.
- Rules met: Floor (no size below 24px changed), Primary (a toggle never shares the primary's accent; in a destructive question the highlighted answer is the safe one), Destructive (the removals tinted, last and apart), Gaps (the canvas menu's actions a chunk of their own), Learned place (no menu order or key changed), Posture (unchanged).
- Not done here (the Canvas view and the layers files are being rewritten): the audit's L1 to L18, the Mask editor's and the paint panel's toggles (D7), D4 (one button component) and D5 (one keymap).

### 2026-11-22 areas: header, border handle, View > Areas (epic #217 slice 1)

- Control: the area header `src/workspace/AreaHeader.qml` (`areaHeader-<id>`; the view-type menu `areaType-<id>` and its list `areaViewMenu`, `areaSplitRow-<id>`, `areaSplitColumn-<id>`, `areaClose-<id>`), the border `src/workspace/AreaHandle.qml` (`areaHandle`), the body's host `areaHost`, the Canvas area `canvasArea`, and the View menu's **Areas** submenu (`area.split-row`, `area.split-column`, `area.close`, `area.view-graph`, `area.view-canvas`, `view.reset-layout`).
- Old: the window's body is one area showing the Graph, with nothing to split it.
- New: View > Areas (after Focus mode) and `Ctrl+\` split the active area. With two or more areas each has a 7u (28px) header in `popup` with a hairline foot: at the start the view menu (the view's glyph, its name, a chevron; 6u tall, as wide as its text), at the end split side by side and split top and bottom (6u = 24px squares, a drawn box with its new border), a 2u gap, then close (24px, the `x` glyph, the danger tint on hover). The active area's foot is a 2px accent line and its name is in the text color, the others' in the label color. Each border is a 24px press target centered on it, as long as the branch, drawn as a 1px hairline (2px accent while hovered, pressed or focused), with the split cursor; double click or Enter shares equally, the arrows move it 2u (Shift 10u). The default window (one area) has none of these items (`tests/workspace/data/default-window.txt`, unchanged).
- Why: the type menu is where Blender puts it, at the header's start, where the eye starts reading the area (Learned place); split and close are rare, so they are small and at the far end, away from the menu (Hick: one decision per chunk). The border must be found with a pointer that is moving fast toward a thin line, so its target is 24px though it draws 1px (Fitts). The header hides in focus mode with the other bars so the views can take the whole window (Posture: the canvas stays sovereign).
- Rules met: Floor (header buttons 24px; the view menu 24px tall; the border's target 24px across: it reaches 12px into each side, under the headers' buttons, which the headers keep 3u clear of their ends, so no button loses presses to it and no 24px circle on a button meets the border's target there), Primary (none: a header is chrome; the view menu is the first control), Destructive (close is its own chunk, 2u from the splits against their 0 gap; closing an area loses no data, Reset layout and a split bring an area back, the Canvas it showed stays in the area tree's state), Gaps (0 inside the split chunk, 2u before close; 3u from the header ends), Learned place (the View menu's rows keep their order; Areas is appended after Focus mode; the keys are new and take nothing from 12.4), Posture (the default window is unchanged; areas are opt-in power).

The window's body is now an area tree of one area showing the Graph (`src/workspace/AreaNode.qml`, `objectName: "areas"`, not an item); the canvas (`canvas`) is that area's view, declared where it was. `src/workspace/AreaPlaceholder.qml` (`areaPlaceholder`, a line naming a view type this build does not have) is drawn only in a split tree, which the window cannot show yet.
