### 2026-11-25 Privacy question and the "Runs on" mark for services off this machine (#404, epic #409)

- Control: `src/run/RemoteConsentQuestion.qml` (`remoteConsentAsk`, one `remoteConsentService` line per service, answers `remoteConsentNo` and `remoteConsentYes`), the node face's mark `runsOnMark` in `src/canvas/CanvasNode.qml`, and the App view's existing `appRunsOn` line.
- Old: a run routed to any provider instance sent its inputs there without a word; the App view said "Runs on <host>" only for a paired helper.
- New: before a run that would send pictures, masks, audio or prompts to a service off this machine, one `Question` names each service (name and host) and what leaves it, with **No, cancel the run** and **Yes, send it**; the yes is kept per service. A node routed there shows "Runs on <service> (<host>)" in the label color above its face, and the App view's line says the same.
- Rules met: Primary (Yes the one accent fill, not the default: the question opens on No), Keys (Tab reaches Yes, Enter on it sends; Enter on No and Escape cancel the run wherever the focus is; the whole question works without a pointer), Destructive (nothing is removed; No only cancels the run), Gaps (3u between lines, 2u between answers, as every Question), Words ("Yes, send it" and "No, cancel the run" say what the answer does), Right-to-left (the content mirrors as every Question's), Floor (the mark uses `captionSize`, quiet, never over a chip: it sits above the card, the chips below).
- Decision (changed after review, confirm): the focus opens on No, not on the primary Yes, because a stray Enter must never grant consent to send data off the machine; the primary stays visually Yes. Escape answers No instead of doing nothing, because the run is waiting on the answer and a closed question that left it waiting would hang the queue.
- Not checked by a person: the question and the mark in a real window.

### 2026-11-25 App view: the "…" menu opened as a 4px strip

- Control: `src/canvas/widgets/WidgetMenu.qml` (`implicitWidth`), so the App view's `appMoreMenu` (`src/apps/AppStage.qml`).
- Old: `WidgetMenu` had no width of its own; every caller set one except `appMoreMenu`, which opened as a 4px strip under `appMore` (owner, with a screenshot).
- New: a caller that sets no width gets the widest entry's text and detail plus the row's insets, at least 32u. Callers that set a width keep it.

### 2026-11-25 App view: dragging the inputs panel's border no longer shakes

- Control: `appPanelHandle` in `src/apps/AppPage.qml` (its `border`); `src/kit/KitSegmented.qml` (the thumb, `kitSegmentedThumb`).
- Old: the border was read from the panel's place, which the row layout updates only at its next polish; two pointer moves before it measured from the old border, the drag was applied twice and the panel overshot and came back (owner's recording). Every KitSegmented thumb animated its x and width, so on a resize it trailed the drag and stretched over the next option (Seed, Run again).
- New: the border comes from the same widths that place the panel, so the panel edge stays under the pointer. The thumb animates only its slot: a new choice slides, a resize is followed at once.
- Rules met: Learned place (the border follows the pointer as every area border does), Motion (motion only for a change of state, never for a resize).
- Not changed: a Run again row still stacks its label above the choices when the panel is too narrow for both side by side (KitRow, owner 2026-11-25); that switch is a jump by design.

### 2026-11-25 Packs and Folders pages: the Providers treatment (owner)

- Control: `src/settings/PacksPage.qml` (`packSearch`, `packsUpdateAll`, `packList`, `pack-<id>` rows with `packState-<id>`, `packTrust-<id>`, `packInherited-<id>`, `packWarning-<id>`, `packSecret*-<id>-<name>`, `packUpdate-<id>`, `packRemove-<id>`, `libraryList`, `libraryGroup-<id>`, `packAddRow` with `packLocation`, `packRef`, `packAdd`) and the new `src/settings/FoldersPage.qml` (`folderList`, `folderRow-<key>`, `folderBody-<key>`; the fields keep their names `output_folder`, `inputs_folder`, `<key>-add`, `-remove-N`, `-holds-N-…`, `-found-N`, `-inherited-…`, `downloadFolder-<path>`) with `PathsList.qml` (was `PathsSetting.qml`, the body of the two folder-list rows); `KitDisclosureRow` gains `statusWidth` and `detailElide`.
- Old: Packs was a card per pack with its buttons, secrets and problems all showing, then the library, then an Add form, Update all, a status block. Folders was five stacked Setting cards.
- New: Packs is a search (Ctrl+F) and Update all, then one 10u row per pack (glyph, name, version, trusted / not trusted / disabled, a Trust… chip in the status's place while it is not trusted, chevron); one open at a time with its folder and node count, "Inherited from Default" and its warning, its load problems (copy button), its keys, and Update (left) and Remove… (right, danger) in the tray's footer; the library's groups are compact rows with Rename… and Remove… (the confirm swaps the chips for Keep and Remove, as before); "Add a pack" is a closed row holding the folder or git address, the tag or commit and Add… (open while no pack is installed); the question stays at the top, and the busy line, message, failure and log under the page. Folders is five rows in one group (Output folder, Inputs folder, Extra model folders, Download models into, Extra pack folders), each showing the folder in effect (middle-elided) and Default / Custom (or the count of folders), one open at a time with its note and controls in a tray; the folder lists keep Remove (round x), what a model folder holds, the inherited rows and Add / Choose….
- Why: the owner's words ("do the same treatment to Packs and to Folders"); the same list, row, badge and tray as Providers, so a settings page reads one way.
- Rules met: Floor (rows 10u, chips and fields 7u/8u as Providers), Primary (Add… is the one accent of its row; none elsewhere), Destructive (Remove… last in a pack's footer, danger, the question still asks; the folder x is a plain remove as before), Gaps (the list 3u under the search, the library 5u after the list, the add row 5u after it), Keys (Tab stops on one header per list; arrows, Home and End step headers, Down from the last pack goes to Add a pack; Enter/Space/Right open, Left/Escape close; Ctrl+F focuses the packs search; the focus returns to the header of the row a change rebuilt), Right-to-left (rows mirror as Providers), States (disabled, not trusted and busy read in the row, not in colour alone).
- Taken without the owner (each to confirm): "models" in the request read as the Providers page (the coordinator's reading; the same list treatment is what the owner saw) (medium-high); Folders gets no search, five rows do not earn one (high), and has no Ctrl+F; Packs gets one, the list is as long as the packs installed, bundled ones included (high); Trust… stays in the header, not in the tray, because a not-trusted pack is the page's one thing to act on (medium-high); an inherited pack says so in its status ("inherited, not trusted") and in the trust question, and the badge and warning are in the open row (medium-high); Update all moved beside the search (medium-high); the library stays its own small list under the packs, not mixed into them (medium-high); a pack that did not load is keyed by its folder (it has no id) and is named by its folder; the secret's state is a badge, not coloured text; the Folders rows keep the old objectNames, so each row must be open before its fields can be reached (tests updated).
- Not checked by a person: a real window (only offscreen pictures, dark and light, wide and narrow); right to left; a long list of packs (about 40 bundled and installed); a pack with a long name or version; the Choose… dialog (the existing test only checks that it opens).
- Review fixes (same day): rows are keyed by the pack's path (ids can repeat); the version is in the tray's first line; a secret's description shows whole; the Trust… chip is a Tab stop only on the row that is the list's stop; Add… is not accent (the question's Yes is); fields never wider than the tray; the Folders status column is measured; `PacksModel::setSecret/clearSecret` take the pack's path, so a key binds to that pack's source.

### 2026-11-26 First run: find your model folders (#619, owner)

- Control: `src/settings/ModelSetupSheet.qml` (`modelSetupSheet`; places `modelSetupPlace-<id>`, rows `modelSetupRow-<n>` with `modelSetupToggle-<n>`, `modelSetupAdd`, `modelSetupDownloadChoice`, `modelSetupSkip`, `modelSetupPrimary`), opened by itself once at the first run and by Settings > Folders > Extra model folders > "Find model folders…" (`model_paths-find`) and the Get ready "Change model folder…" menu ("Find model folders…", `getReadyFindFolders`).
- Old: nothing. A new install had no model folder, so every template offered a download, even for a person with a ComfyUI folder of 400 GB.
- New: two screens, one at a time, in one Moss `KitPanel` (680 px at most, modal, a `KitHero`). The consent screen says why and what is read (folder names and file sizes: nothing is opened, changed or sent), lists every place it would look with a `KitToggle` each (the usual install folders that exist, `~` 4 levels, `/mnt`, `/media/$USER`, `/run/media/$USER`; a missing one is not listed), then Add a folder…, Skip and Search. Only Search searches; a place left off is never touched. The results show what was found as `KitRow`s (the tool, the path cut in the middle, the size and the three biggest kinds) with a toggle each, "In use" and "Inherited from Default" badges, "Add a folder…", "New downloads" with a dropdown (each picked writable folder and Fugue's own, with the room left) and Use N folders. Nothing found says so and offers Done.
- Why: the owner's rule that a search must never start unannounced; the same tools (ComfyUI Desktop, Stability Matrix) ask at first run but neither finds models already there. Downloads default to the biggest writable folder picked (the owner), so a download lands beside the collection and ComfyUI sees it too; a short disk keeps the choice and says "Only 12 GB free there." in the error colour.
- Rules met: Primary (one accent fill: Search, then Use N folders or Done), Order (Skip first, the primary last at the right, D9), Floor (Chips 7u, toggles 7u presses), Keys (opening focuses Skip, on the consent screen and while it looks, then the primary; Tab walks the toggles in reading order, then Add a folder…, the download dropdown, Skip, the primary; Up and Down move between toggles, Space flips one, Enter anywhere but on a chip runs the primary, Escape is Skip and the focus goes back to what opened it), Words (an ellipsis only where a dialog follows), Gaps (the kit's).
- Taken without the owner (each to confirm): the consent screen focuses Skip, not Search, so Enter never starts a search by habit (high); "Show %n more" is a `KitRow` with a chip, not a `KitDisclosureRow`, so the switches stay in one Tab and arrow order (medium-high); the long consent text is a paragraph under the header, the hero keeps its one-line subtitle (high); a folder in no tool's layout and with no models is left out (a source tree's `model/` and `docs/`); Windows' own folders (`Windows`, `$RECYCLE.BIN`, `System Volume Information`) are not walked (high); Add a folder… sits under the list, before the download choice, as the design's Tab order says.
- Not checked by a person: a real window (offscreen pictures at 680 and 480 px, dark theme; `~/Pictures/fugue-first-run/`); right to left; the portal's folder chooser behind Add a folder…; a spinning drive or an NTFS mount (the caps are tested with an injected clock and entry limits, and one read-only run on the real machine).

### 2026-11-25 Load Models: the Tokenizer input removed, Get ready offers the tokenizer (#544, owner)

- Control: Load Models (`fugue/load-models` 1.2.0, no `tokenizer` input) and the Get ready sheet (`getReadyModel-qwen3.5_tokenizer.json`, from `tests/canvas/test_get_ready_sheet.cpp` `aMossWorkflowWithoutTheTokenizerOffersItsDownload`).
- Old: a MOSS Image run failed with "not in your tokenizers folder"; the fix was a Tokenizer drop-down on Load Models that the owner had not noticed ("was not obvious I needed that").
- New: no Tokenizer input. The tokenizer is a companion file of the model: a workflow whose Load Models holds a MOSS model without it on disk shows the Get ready banner on open, and the sheet lists qwen3.5_tokenizer.json (13 MB, Apache-2.0, huggingface.co) like a model file, with the one yes that downloads it. Load Models' check warns on the Diffusion model input; a run that still lacks it fails `model_missing` and the hint says Get ready. A workflow saved with the old input opens and runs; the value is ignored.
- Why: the owner's words; a file the model cannot work without is not a choice (Hick: one fewer control on the node), and the place people already look for what is missing is Get ready.
- Rules met: Primary (the sheet's Install is still the one accent), Gaps and Keys unchanged (the sheet's own rows). Nothing new to lay out: a control left out.
- Not checked by a person: a real window (offscreen picture only); a MOSS model that is only on a paired host does not show its tokenizer in Get ready (its header is read here).

### 2026-11-26 Use what you have: Use my file or Download, per model (#605, #616; replaces the first #605 row)

- Control: Get ready (`getReadyChoice-<name>`, a `KitSegmented`, in `GetReadyRow.qml`; the primary `getReadyAll`; `ReadinessSheet::setChoice`), the template card (`templateStatus-<id>`, `templateReadyGlyph-<id>`, `templateDownload-<id>` in `TemplatesDialog.qml`) and its details (`templateDetailsDownload`), the Needs panel (`appNeedUse-<key>`, `appNeedsAll`), the node warnings card (`useOnDisk-<input>`, the badge `warningsBadge` as a Tab stop), the banner's `noticeUndo`, the App view's `appUseUndo-<hash>`.
- Old: a card that said "Ready" opened Get ready and its primary downloaded 8.7 GB with the file on disk; the offer was a legacy button of about 60 characters inside a disclosure row, it acted before the yes, hashed a 9 GB file with no sign of life, and left the sheet on screen with Install as the primary (the owner's "the button did nothing").
- New (owner decisions 2026-11-26: preselect Use my file; a card ready only through the person's files opens with them, no sheet):
  - Rule: a model with a file of the same weights on disk has two answers, **Use my file** (the default) or **Download**; nothing happens before the surface's yes, and the choice can be undone.
  - Get ready: a `Use my file | Download <size>` segmented choice per matched row, "You have <file> (same model, INT8)." above it, `check` and "on disk" for a file, `download` and the size for a download. The headline, the totals and the Destination follow the choices (no Destination while nothing downloads). The one primary reads **Install** while anything is installed, trusted, set up or downloaded, and **Use my file** or **Use my %n files** when only the person's files are left. The yes reads the chosen files first, each row says "Reading your file… 42%" (the hash's own progress, per row, not global), then the sheet goes on with the rest, or closes by itself when nothing is left. A file that cannot be read fails its row, which is offered again with Download chosen; the failure message goes when the choice changes. Cancel or Escape discards the choices (and drops a read in progress). Enter on a choice runs the primary (`KitSegmented.keepsEnter` false).
  - Template card and details: every way of opening (click, Enter, the details' Open, the Apps shelf, the menu's templates) honours the files: a card that is Ready only through them (`opensWithFiles`) opens straight away with them, no sheet; the banner says "Opened with your file <file> in place of <name> (same model, <tier>)." with Undo. The card reads "Ready · uses your INT8 file" with a `check`; a mixed card reads "Models missing: 1 of 3 · you have 1 of them" and its Download counts only the uncovered models. The details' secondary **Download <size>…** (a bordered `KitChip`) opens the template as it is with Get ready on Download.
  - Needs panel: per row [Show] [Download] [**Use my file**] (toned, last, never the primary; Download is a glyph chip beside it because the panel is narrow); the header chip is **Use my %n files** when every model is covered (applied at once, no sheet), **Get ready…** when some are, and Download all never fetches a covered model; the headline counts only downloads, then "You have %n on disk.". A row reads its own file only.
  - Node warnings card: hovered, a line says "Use my file: <file> (<same model, tier>). Click the warning to keep this card up."; kept up, **Use my file** is a button (the canvas keeps its own look) that stays and reads "Reading your file… %" while the file is hashed; the badge is a Tab stop, Enter or Space keeps the card up with the focus on its first button, Tab moves between offers, Enter presses, Escape closes and gives the focus back to the canvas.
  - App view and banner: the Undo of `appUses` carries a tip "Back to <name>, which needs a download (<size>)"; the missing-models banner adds "You already have %n of them in another file; Get ready… can use it with no download."
- Why: the owner's #616: the file was there, the screen still asked for the download. The explicit yes is the sheet (or the card he clicked, which already says Ready), either answer is one key away, and the expensive target is never the easy one (Primary, Hick).
- Rules met: Primary (one accent per surface: Install or Use my file; the Needs chip is toned), Floor (the segmented control and chips are 7u and 5.5u options), Words (names the file, says the tier), Keys (the choice is one Tab stop with Left and Right, Enter goes to the primary, Escape cancels, the badge is reachable), Undo (graph: one step; card open: the banner's Undo; App view: its own), One calm look (`KitSegmented`, `KitChip`, `KitGroup`; `ThemedButton` stays only on the canvas card).
- Convention named: Photoshop's and ComfyUI's missing-model dialogs give no choice that keeps the file; the choice per row with a default and an undo is ours. Deviation: Download is a glyph in the Needs row (the panel is 320px wide).
- Decisions taken without the owner (each to confirm): the card for a template that needs a pack still opens Get ready (the sheet has more to ask); a card whose models are covered but a host serves another keeps today's rule; Undo of the opened-with banner is the tab's own undo step (Ctrl+Z also works) and shows only while that line is the banner's and its tab is shown; hover offers are kept 2 s per node (`onDiskOffers`).
- Not checked: that Tab alone reaches the warnings badge from the canvas (the window's Tab is also Add a node by search; the test focuses the badge and drives the rest by keys); by a person: a real window and a real 9 GB file (offscreen pictures, a small bf16 header file whose hash is read on the worker); the pictures are in `~/Pictures/fugue-use-ui/`.

### 2026-11-26 Use my file by itself: the setting (#618; follows the #616 row)

- Control: Settings > General, `src/settings/SettingsPanel.qml` "When a model is missing but the same model is on disk" (`useOnDisk`), a `Choices` of two (Use my file, Ask me), after Sampler preview. Writes `use_on_disk` in settings.json; Use my file is the default and leaves the file.
- Old: every covered model waited for a click (the sheet's yes, the card, the Needs chip), even for the person who always says yes.
- New (owner decision 2026-11-26): with Use my file the click is gone. A template or an app opens with its covered models in place and Get ready only for the rest (a template that needs a pack still opens the sheet); a workflow file or a tab shown later takes its files once they are read (the sheet of a tab just opened waits for that); Get ready, the Needs panel and the node card find nothing covered to offer. One banner line names the file and the tier ("Used your file <file> in place of <name> (same model, <tier>)."), and its Undo is one step in the graph (the App view's line has its own). With Ask me, the #616 flow is unchanged.
- Why: the choice with a safe default and an undo is the pattern the owner wants (Primary, Undo); a swap is never silent, and the workflow file is written only on save.
- Rules met: Primary (no extra control on any surface), Words (the line names file, model and tier), Undo (one step, not undone by the next check), Floor (the setting is a two-option `Choices` like its neighbours).
- Decisions taken without the owner (each to confirm): the setting is a general preference (inherited by a project like `new_seed`), key `use_on_disk` with values `use` and `ask`; the template details' Download button and a Get ready the person opened by hand are not auto-applied; a model whose file could not be read, or whose swap was undone, is not tried again for that tab; Run does not read files itself, it relies on the swap done at open (a run started while a file is still being read runs without it); the CLI's `validate` follows the setting too, so it answers what `run` would do.
- Not checked: by a person: the banner and its Undo in a real window with a real 9 GB file; the Settings row's look.

### 2026-11-26 CUDA helper notice: wording for a host without pacman, and shown in an AppImage (#334)

- Control: `helperNotice`, `helperNoticeCopy` and `helperNoticeDismiss` in `src/Main.qml`, unchanged in place, size and order.
- Old: one line, "Faster generation on your NVIDIA card: install fugue-helper-cuda", and a button "Copy sudo pacman -S fugue-helper-cuda", on every host; in an AppImage with a Vulkan helper it never showed, because `AppRun` sets `FUGUE_HELPER` and the notice read that as the person's choice.
- New: on a host that runs the packaged build (pacman, not an AppImage) the words are as before. Elsewhere the line reads "Faster generation on your NVIDIA card: download the CUDA helper" and the button "Copy the install command", which copies `curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bmaltais/fugue/main/install.sh | bash -s -- --helper-only` (the command is a full pipeline, so the button says what the click puts on the clipboard instead of showing it). `AppRun` now marks its own pick with `FUGUE_HELPER_FROM_APPRUN=1`, so only a `FUGUE_HELPER` the person set hides the notice.
- Why: an AppImage host has no pacman, so the pacman command would not run; the install script is the same helper download `docs/helper.md` documents. The notice's point is unchanged, one action then the ×.
- Rules met: Floor (button and × unchanged), Primary (Copy stays the row's one highlighted control), Gaps (8px inside the row, as before), Posture (transient, asks nothing). Words: the label names the action, not the mechanism.
- Not checked by a person: a real AppImage on an NVIDIA host (`tests/engine/test_helper_notice.cpp` covers the rule, `packaging/appimage/test-apprun.sh` the mark); the notice is not in the offscreen picture tests.

### 2026-11-26 New project: the name survives, and the project gets a folder of its own (owner report)

- Control: `newProjectName`, `newProjectFolder`, `newProjectChoose` and the new line `newProjectPath` in `src/settings/ProjectsDialog.qml`; the buttons are unchanged in place, size and order.
- Old: the name typed was lost when the person went to the folder (the portal's picker takes the window's focus, and a `Field` gives its stored `value` back on focus loss; these two fields stored nothing). The Folder was the project's root itself, so a new folder could only be had from the picker, and neither the portal's nor Qt's own picker is certain to offer one (Qt's has no "new folder").
- New: both fields keep what is typed (the dialog's `newNameText` and `newFolderText` follow every keystroke), and the field is now "Folder to make it in": the project gets a folder of its own named from its name inside it (`<folder>/<slug>`, `-2`, `-3` when taken), made with its parents on Create. A line under the field shows the full path ("The project will be made at ..."), recomputed as the name or folder changes. Empty keeps the default place.
- Why (medium-high): VS Code's and Godot's new-project flows take a parent folder and a name and make the folder; Blender's browser has a create-directory button only because it browses. A new project's folder is almost always new, so no picker button is needed, and it does not depend on which backend answers. An existing folder to use as the root stays possible from the CLI (`fugue project new --in`) and `Open folder…` for one that is already a project.
- Rules met: Primary (no new control), Words (the line says what will be made and where), Floor (field and Choose… unchanged), Gaps (a caption line, as the notes).
- Not checked by a person: the portal picker in the real window.

### 2026-11-26 Compare node: the plain wheel zooms the picture while the node is selected (#437; owner decision)

- Control: `src/canvas/CanvasNode.qml`, the Compare Images face (`compareViewer`, `wheelModifiers`); no control added or moved.
- Old: only Alt+wheel zoomed the picture on the face; a plain wheel always moved the graph, even over a selected node.
- New: while the node is selected, the wheel over its picture zooms the picture (plain, or with a key held, Alt or Ctrl); not selected, the plain wheel stays the graph's and Alt+wheel zooms the picture as before.
- Why: selecting the node is the person's statement that they are working on its picture; the Large view and the App stage already zoom with the plain wheel. An unselected node keeps the wheel so the graph can still be panned across a Compare node.
- Rules met: Learned place (nothing moved), Posture (selection already means "this is the one acted on").
- Not checked by a person: a real mouse in a real window (the window test sends wheel events).
